It's just... it's a lot to deal with.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the routing system is actually capable of tracking you specifically and sending you personal messages?\n\nPatient: Yes, I know it is. It's... it's not just a computer to me anymore. I know for a fact it's been marking my moves out on the floor. The messages come right through the scanners, and they're doing it to push me out because I'm one of the old hands. I wouldn't be sitting here saying this if I wasn't completely sure about what I'm seeing.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any other situations outside of work where you felt like something or someone was specifically targeting or monitoring you?\n\nPatient: No, not really. It's just at the warehouse. When I go home or... or out to the store, I don't get that feeling. It's really just that routing system at work. That's the only thing that's been doing it.\n\nDoctor: How long have you believed the routing system was tracking you and sending you personal messages?\n\nPatient: Well, the whole thing with the scanners... and the voices that come with it... it's been going on for a while now. I'd say it's been about a year and eight months since it all started.\n\nDoctor: You mentioned voices that come with the scanners, can you tell me more about what these voices say?\n\nPatient: They sound exactly like the dispatch radio. They call out my specific operator number. And... well, I know I just said the tracking stuff only happens at the warehouse, but the voices are a little different. Even when I'm at home, sitting out in my garage with everything completely shut off, I hear those dispatch voices clear as day. They keep saying that I'm dangerous, or... or they'll tell me that security is coming for me.\n\nDoctor: Can you hear those dispatch voices through anything else besides the scanners at work, like a radio or your own thoughts, when you're not near any warehouse equipment?\n\nPatient: Well, like I just said, I don't even need a radio or a scanner to hear them. I can just be sitting out in my garage with everything completely shut off... no electronics running at all. And it doesn't feel like it's just my own thoughts. It sounds like real voices out loud, clear as day. It's just that same dispatch voice calling my operator number, telling me I'm dangerous and that security is on their way to get me.\n\nDoctor: Do the voices you hear ever tell you to do something specific?\n\nPatient: No, they don't really tell me to do things. They just... they just keep repeating the same stuff. Calling my number, saying I'm dangerous, saying security is coming. They don't give me orders or tell me to act a certain way, it's just... it's just those warnings, over and over.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever have any doubts that the routing system and the voices are actually targeting you?\n\nPatient: No, I don't have any doubts. I... I know exactly what's happening. That routing system at the warehouse, it isn't just a normal computer to me anymore. I know for a fact it has been marking my moves. I wouldn't be sitting here saying this if I wasn't completely sure about what I'm seeing.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any other situations outside of work where you felt like something or someone was specifically targeting or monitoring you?\n\nPatient: No, not really. It's just at the warehouse. When I go home or... or out to the store, I don't get that feeling. It's really just that routing system at work. That's the only thing that's been doing it.\n\nDoctor: How long have you believed the routing system was tracking you and sending you personal messages?\n\nPatient: Well, the whole thing with the scanners... and the voices that come with it... it's been going on for a while now. I'd say it's been about a year and eight months since it all started.\n\nDoctor: You mentioned voices that come with the scanners, can you tell me more about what these voices say?\n\nPatient: They sound exactly like the dispatch radio. They call out my specific operator number. And... well, I know I just said the tracking stuff only happens at the warehouse, but the voices are a little different. Even when I'm at home, sitting out in my garage with everything completely shut off, I hear those dispatch voices clear as day. They keep saying that I'm dangerous, or... or they'll tell me that security is coming for me.\n\nDoctor: Can you hear those dispatch voices through anything else besides the scanners at work, like a radio or your own thoughts, when you're not near any warehouse equipment?\n\nPatient: Well, like I just said, I don't even need a radio or a scanner to hear them. I can just be sitting out in my garage with everything completely shut off... no electronics running at all. And it doesn't feel like it's just my own thoughts. It sounds like real voices out loud, clear as day. It's just that same dispatch voice calling my operator number, telling me I'm dangerous and that security is on their way to get me.\n\nDoctor: Do the voices you hear ever tell you to do something specific?\n\nPatient: No, they don't really tell me to do things. They just... they just keep repeating the same stuff. Calling my number, saying I'm dangerous, saying security is coming. They don't give me orders or tell me to act a certain way, it's just... it's just those warnings, over and over.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever have any doubts that the routing system and the voices are actually targeting you?\n\nPatient: No, I don't have any doubts. I... I know exactly what's happening. That routing system at the warehouse, it isn't just a normal computer to me anymore. I know for a fact it has been marking my moves. It's sending those messages through the scanners on purpose to push me out, just because I'm one of the old hands there. So, no... there's really no doubt in my mind.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find it hard to express your thoughts or feelings in a way that makes sense to others?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, I feel like I'm making sense right now, aren't I? People usually understand me just fine. My thoughts are... they're clear to me, and I just say what I'm thinking. I don't feel like my words get jumbled up or anything like that, if that's what you mean.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like your thoughts are racing or hard to keep track of when you're not hearing the voices?\n\nPatient: No, my thoughts don't really race. Even when the voices are quiet, my mind just feels heavy, I guess. I don't have trouble keeping track of what I'm thinking, it's just... I'm usually just thinking about how tired I am, or worrying about the warehouse. But they don't speed up or get out of control.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find yourself jumping from one topic to another while talking, without really finishing your thoughts?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think I do that. When I talk, I just... I say what I need to say. I don't really jump around. If I start talking about something, I finish it. I'm not the most talkative guy anyway, especially lately with how down I've been feeling, but when I do speak, I stick to the point.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like you're having trouble organizing your thoughts when you're trying to explain something to someone?\n\nPatient: No, I don't have trouble with that. If I have to explain something to someone... well, I just tell them. I don't get confused or lose my place. Like I was saying before, I don't talk a whole lot right now anyway because I'm just so drained, but when I do have to explain something, like how to move a load at the warehouse or whatever, it comes out fine. My thoughts stay pretty organized.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. If it did not change, leave \"note\" empty.\n- If the candidates have narrowed to one and you are confident enough that no further questioning is needed, return true for is_final.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"candidates\": [\"<ICD-10 code>\", \"...\"], \"note\": \"<why the list changed, or empty>\", \"is_final\": <true or false>}\n"

I wouldn't be sitting here saying this if I wasn't completely sure about what I'm seeing.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any other situations outside of work where you felt like something or someone was specifically targeting or monitoring you?\n\nPatient: No, not really. It's just at the warehouse. When I go home or... or out to the store, I don't get that feeling. It's really just that routing system at work. That's the only thing that's been doing it.\n\nDoctor: How long have you believed the routing system was tracking you and sending you personal messages?\n\nPatient: Well, the whole thing with the scanners... and the voices that come with it... it's been going on for a while now. I'd say it's been about a year and eight months since it all started.\n\nDoctor: You mentioned voices that come with the scanners, can you tell me more about what these voices say?\n\nPatient: They sound exactly like the dispatch radio. They call out my specific operator number. And... well, I know I just said the tracking stuff only happens at the warehouse, but the voices are a little different. Even when I'm at home, sitting out in my garage with everything completely shut off, I hear those dispatch voices clear as day. They keep saying that I'm dangerous, or... or they'll tell me that security is coming for me.\n\nDoctor: Can you hear those dispatch voices through anything else besides the scanners at work, like a radio or your own thoughts, when you're not near any warehouse equipment?\n\nPatient: Well, like I just said, I don't even need a radio or a scanner to hear them. I can just be sitting out in my garage with everything completely shut off... no electronics running at all. And it doesn't feel like it's just my own thoughts. It sounds like real voices out loud, clear as day. It's just that same dispatch voice calling my operator number, telling me I'm dangerous and that security is on their way to get me.\n\nDoctor: Do the voices you hear ever tell you to do something specific?\n\nPatient: No, they don't really tell me to do things. They just... they just keep repeating the same stuff. Calling my number, saying I'm dangerous, saying security is coming. They don't give me orders or tell me to act a certain way, it's just... it's just those warnings, over and over.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever have any doubts that the routing system and the voices are actually targeting you?\n\nPatient: No, I don't have any doubts. I... I know exactly what's happening. That routing system at the warehouse, it isn't just a normal computer to me anymore. I know for a fact it has been marking my moves. It's sending those messages through the scanners on purpose to push me out, just because I'm one of the old hands there. So, no... there's really no doubt in my mind.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find it hard to express your thoughts or feelings in a way that makes sense to others?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, I feel like I'm making sense right now, aren't I? People usually understand me just fine. My thoughts are... they're clear to me, and I just say what I'm thinking. I don't feel like my words get jumbled up or anything like that, if that's what you mean.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like your thoughts are racing or hard to keep track of when you're not hearing the voices?\n\nPatient: No, my thoughts don't really race. Even when the voices are quiet, my mind just feels heavy, I guess. I don't have trouble keeping track of what I'm thinking, it's just... I'm usually just thinking about how tired I am, or worrying about the warehouse. But they don't speed up or get out of control.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find yourself jumping from one topic to another while talking, without really finishing your thoughts?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think I do that. When I talk, I just... I say what I need to say. I don't really jump around. If I start talking about something, I finish it. I'm not the most talkative guy anyway, especially lately with how down I've been feeling, but when I do speak, I stick to the point.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like you're having trouble organizing your thoughts when you're trying to explain something to someone?\n\nPatient: No, I don't have trouble with that. If I have to explain something to someone... well, I just tell them. I don't get confused or lose my place. Like I was saying before, I don't talk a whole lot right now anyway because I'm just so drained, but when I do have to explain something, like how to move a load at the warehouse or whatever, it comes out fine. My thoughts stay pretty organized.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever have any periods of time where you don't believe the routing system is tracking you or that the voices are real?\n\nPatient: No, not really. Even when things aren't quite as bad... like, there was a stretch a while back where my mood had eased up a bit. I was eating a little more, talking a little more, not feeling quite as heavy. But even then, for several weeks during that time, I was still hearing dispatch calling my number. And I still knew the routing system was tracking me. It doesn't just go away because I'm feeling a little better.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any significant changes in your mood or behavior over the past year and eight months, aside from the feelings related to the routing system and voices?\n\nPatient: Well, yeah. I mean, my mood has been really bad. There was a time when I was at my lowest point... I was barely eating anything, I stopped going to my choir practice, and I just had this overwhelming feeling that I had failed everybody. But even when I was feeling that down, the scanner messages and the dispatch voices... they were happening right along with it. They didn't stop just because I was feeling so depressed.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there are other people or things, besides the routing system, that are involved in plotting against you?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't noticed anything else like that. It's really just the routing system tracking me and the dispatch voices. I don't think anyone else is involved, or at least, I haven't seen any sign of it. It's just the system at the warehouse that I know is monitoring me.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. They keep saying that I'm dangerous, or... or they'll tell me that security is coming for me.\n\nDoctor: Can you hear those dispatch voices through anything else besides the scanners at work, like a radio or your own thoughts, when you're not near any warehouse equipment?\n\nPatient: Well, like I just said, I don't even need a radio or a scanner to hear them. I can just be sitting out in my garage with everything completely shut off... no electronics running at all. And it doesn't feel like it's just my own thoughts. It sounds like real voices out loud, clear as day. It's just that same dispatch voice calling my operator number, telling me I'm dangerous and that security is on their way to get me.\n\nDoctor: Do the voices you hear ever tell you to do something specific?\n\nPatient: No, they don't really tell me to do things. They just... they just keep repeating the same stuff. Calling my number, saying I'm dangerous, saying security is coming. They don't give me orders or tell me to act a certain way, it's just... it's just those warnings, over and over.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever have any doubts that the routing system and the voices are actually targeting you?\n\nPatient: No, I don't have any doubts. I... I know exactly what's happening. That routing system at the warehouse, it isn't just a normal computer to me anymore. I know for a fact it has been marking my moves. It's sending those messages through the scanners on purpose to push me out, just because I'm one of the old hands there. So, no... there's really no doubt in my mind.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find it hard to express your thoughts or feelings in a way that makes sense to others?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, I feel like I'm making sense right now, aren't I? People usually understand me just fine. My thoughts are... they're clear to me, and I just say what I'm thinking. I don't feel like my words get jumbled up or anything like that, if that's what you mean.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like your thoughts are racing or hard to keep track of when you're not hearing the voices?\n\nPatient: No, my thoughts don't really race. Even when the voices are quiet, my mind just feels heavy, I guess. I don't have trouble keeping track of what I'm thinking, it's just... I'm usually just thinking about how tired I am, or worrying about the warehouse. But they don't speed up or get out of control.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find yourself jumping from one topic to another while talking, without really finishing your thoughts?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think I do that. When I talk, I just... I say what I need to say. I don't really jump around. If I start talking about something, I finish it. I'm not the most talkative guy anyway, especially lately with how down I've been feeling, but when I do speak, I stick to the point.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like you're having trouble organizing your thoughts when you're trying to explain something to someone?\n\nPatient: No, I don't have trouble with that. If I have to explain something to someone... well, I just tell them. I don't get confused or lose my place. Like I was saying before, I don't talk a whole lot right now anyway because I'm just so drained, but when I do have to explain something, like how to move a load at the warehouse or whatever, it comes out fine. My thoughts stay pretty organized.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever have any periods of time where you don't believe the routing system is tracking you or that the voices are real?\n\nPatient: No, not really. Even when things aren't quite as bad... like, there was a stretch a while back where my mood had eased up a bit. I was eating a little more, talking a little more, not feeling quite as heavy. But even then, for several weeks during that time, I was still hearing dispatch calling my number. And I still knew the routing system was tracking me. It doesn't just go away because I'm feeling a little better.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any significant changes in your mood or behavior over the past year and eight months, aside from the feelings related to the routing system and voices?\n\nPatient: Well, yeah. I mean, my mood has been really bad. There was a time when I was at my lowest point... I was barely eating anything, I stopped going to my choir practice, and I just had this overwhelming feeling that I had failed everybody. But even when I was feeling that down, the scanner messages and the dispatch voices... they were happening right along with it. They didn't stop just because I was feeling so depressed.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there are other people or things, besides the routing system, that are involved in plotting against you?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't noticed anything else like that. It's really just the routing system tracking me and the dispatch voices. I don't think anyone else is involved, or at least, I haven't seen any sign of it. It's just the system at the warehouse that I know is monitoring me.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find it difficult to stick to one topic when talking to someone?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I mean, I usually just answer what people ask me. I don't really have trouble staying on track when I'm talking... I just say what needs to be said.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Never bundle several symptoms, or several attributes of one symptom, into a single question — e.g. asking whether a symptom is present and how long it has lasted at the same time.\n- If you need to ask about more than one thing, ask the one that tells you the most now and come back to the rest later.\n- Keep the question clear enough for the patient to understand.\n- Respond in English.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"intent\": \"<what you want to find out with this question>\", \"question\": \"<how you actually ask it in the interview>\"}\n"

I... I know exactly what's happening. That routing system at the warehouse, it isn't just a normal computer to me anymore. I know for a fact it has been marking my moves. It's sending those messages through the scanners on purpose to push me out, just because I'm one of the old hands there. So, no... there's really no doubt in my mind.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find it hard to express your thoughts or feelings in a way that makes sense to others?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, I feel like I'm making sense right now, aren't I? People usually understand me just fine. My thoughts are... they're clear to me, and I just say what I'm thinking. I don't feel like my words get jumbled up or anything like that, if that's what you mean.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like your thoughts are racing or hard to keep track of when you're not hearing the voices?\n\nPatient: No, my thoughts don't really race. Even when the voices are quiet, my mind just feels heavy, I guess. I don't have trouble keeping track of what I'm thinking, it's just... I'm usually just thinking about how tired I am, or worrying about the warehouse. But they don't speed up or get out of control.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find yourself jumping from one topic to another while talking, without really finishing your thoughts?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think I do that. When I talk, I just... I say what I need to say. I don't really jump around. If I start talking about something, I finish it. I'm not the most talkative guy anyway, especially lately with how down I've been feeling, but when I do speak, I stick to the point.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like you're having trouble organizing your thoughts when you're trying to explain something to someone?\n\nPatient: No, I don't have trouble with that. If I have to explain something to someone... well, I just tell them. I don't get confused or lose my place. Like I was saying before, I don't talk a whole lot right now anyway because I'm just so drained, but when I do have to explain something, like how to move a load at the warehouse or whatever, it comes out fine. My thoughts stay pretty organized.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever have any periods of time where you don't believe the routing system is tracking you or that the voices are real?\n\nPatient: No, not really. But even then, for several weeks during that time, I was still hearing dispatch calling my number. And I still knew the routing system was tracking me. It doesn't just go away because I'm feeling a little better.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any significant changes in your mood or behavior over the past year and eight months, aside from the feelings related to the routing system and voices?\n\nPatient: Well, yeah. I mean, my mood has been really bad. There was a time when I was at my lowest point... I was barely eating anything, I stopped going to my choir practice, and I just had this overwhelming feeling that I had failed everybody. I don't feel like my words get jumbled up or anything like that, if that's what you mean.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like your thoughts are racing or hard to keep track of when you're not hearing the voices?\n\nPatient: No, my thoughts don't really race. I'm not the most talkative guy anyway, especially lately with how down I've been feeling, but when I do speak, I stick to the point.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like you're having trouble organizing your thoughts when you're trying to explain something to someone?\n\nPatient: No, I don't have trouble with that. My thoughts stay pretty organized.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever have any periods of time where you don't believe the routing system is tracking you or that the voices are real?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I was barely eating anything, I stopped going to my choir practice, and I just had this overwhelming feeling that I had failed everybody. But even when I was feeling that down, the scanner messages and the dispatch voices... they were happening right along with it. They didn't stop just because I was feeling so depressed.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there are other people or things, besides the routing system, that are involved in plotting against you?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't noticed anything else like that. They keep saying that I'm dangerous, or... or they'll tell me that security is coming for me.\n\nDoctor: Can you hear those dispatch voices through anything else besides the scanners at work, like a radio or your own thoughts, when you're not near any warehouse equipment?\n\nPatient: Well, like I just said, I don't even need a radio or a scanner to hear them. I can just be sitting out in my garage with everything completely shut off... no electronics running at all. And it doesn't feel like it's just my own thoughts. It sounds like real voices out loud, clear as day. It's just that same dispatch voice calling my operator number, telling me I'm dangerous and that security is on their way to get me.\n\nDoctor: Do the voices you hear ever tell you to do something specific?\n\nPatient: No, they don't really tell me to do things. They just... they just keep repeating the same stuff. Calling my number, saying I'm dangerous, saying security is coming. They don't give me orders or tell me to act a certain way, it's just... it's just those warnings, over and over.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever have any doubts that the routing system and the voices are actually targeting you?\n\nPatient: No, I don't have any doubts. I... I know exactly what's happening. That routing system at the warehouse, it isn't just a normal computer to me anymore. I know for a fact it has been marking my moves. It's sending those messages through the scanners on purpose to push me out, just because I'm one of the old hands there. So, no... there's really no doubt in my mind.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find it hard to express your thoughts or feelings in a way that makes sense to others?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, I feel like I'm making sense right now, aren't I? People usually understand me just fine. My thoughts are... they're clear to me, and I just say what I'm thinking. I don't feel like my words get jumbled up or anything like that, if that's what you mean.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like your thoughts are racing or hard to keep track of when you're not hearing the voices?\n\nPatient: No, my thoughts don't really race. Even when the voices are quiet, my mind just feels heavy, I guess. I don't have trouble keeping track of what I'm thinking, it's just... I'm usually just thinking about how tired I am, or worrying about the warehouse. But they don't speed up or get out of control.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find yourself jumping from one topic to another while talking, without really finishing your thoughts?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think I do that. When I talk, I just... I say what I need to say. I don't really jump around. If I start talking about something, I finish it. I'm not the most talkative guy anyway, especially lately with how down I've been feeling, but when I do speak, I stick to the point.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like you're having trouble organizing your thoughts when you're trying to explain something to someone?\n\nPatient: No, I don't have trouble with that. If I have to explain something to someone... well, I just tell them. I don't get confused or lose my place. Like I was saying before, I don't talk a whole lot right now anyway because I'm just so drained, but when I do have to explain something, like how to move a load at the warehouse or whatever, it comes out fine. My thoughts stay pretty organized.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever have any periods of time where you don't believe the routing system is tracking you or that the voices are real?\n\nPatient: No, not really. Even when things aren't quite as bad... like, there was a stretch a while back where my mood had eased up a bit. I was eating a little more, talking a little more, not feeling quite as heavy. But even then, for several weeks during that time, I was still hearing dispatch calling my number. And I still knew the routing system was tracking me. It doesn't just go away because I'm feeling a little better.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any significant changes in your mood or behavior over the past year and eight months, aside from the feelings related to the routing system and voices?\n\nPatient: Well, yeah. I mean, my mood has been really bad. There was a time when I was at my lowest point... I was barely eating anything, I stopped going to my choir practice, and I just had this overwhelming feeling that I had failed everybody. But even when I was feeling that down, the scanner messages and the dispatch voices... they were happening right along with it. They didn't stop just because I was feeling so depressed.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there are other people or things, besides the routing system, that are involved in plotting against you?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't noticed anything else like that. It's really just the routing system tracking me and the dispatch voices. I don't think anyone else is involved, or at least, I haven't seen any sign of it. It's just the system at the warehouse that I know is monitoring me.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find it difficult to stick to one topic when talking to someone?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I mean, I usually just answer what people ask me. I don't really have trouble staying on track when I'm talking... I just say what needs to be said.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find yourself having trouble putting your thoughts into words when trying to explain something?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, I think I explain things just fine. If I know what I want to say, I just say it. I don't really get stuck trying to find the words or anything like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of time where you felt extremely happy or irritable for no reason, and your thoughts were racing?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't experienced anything like that. I definitely don't get extremely happy for no reason. And my thoughts don't really race around. I just... no, I haven't had any periods of time like what you're describing.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find it hard to get your thoughts across to others in a way that they can understand?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I mean, if I'm talking to someone, they seem to understand me alright. I just tell them what I'm thinking, plain and simple. I haven't noticed people looking confused or telling me they don't get what I'm saying."
